degree of freedom; opportunity."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["The region of program source code in which a given identifier is meaningful, or a given object can be accessed."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"],"topics":["computing","engineering","mathematics","natural-sciences","physical-sciences","programming","sciences"]},{"glosses":["The shortest sub-wff of which a given instance of a logical connective is a part."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"],"topics":["human-sciences","logic","mathematics","philosophy","sciences"]},{"examples":[{"text":"the scope of an adverb","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The region of an utterance to which some modifying element applies."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"],"topics":["human-sciences","linguistics","sciences"]},{"alt_of":[{"word":"any word ending in -scope"},{"word":"such as endoscope"},{"word":"periscope"},{"word":"telescope"},{"word":"microscope"},{"word":"oscilloscope"},{"word":"and so on"}],"glosses":["Ellipsis of any word ending in -scope, such as endoscope, periscope, telescope, microscope, oscilloscope, and so on."],"tags":["abbreviation","alt-of","countable","ellipsis","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["Any medical procedure that ends in the suffix -scopy, such as endoscopy, colonoscopy, bronchoscopy, etc."],"tags":["colloquial","countable","uncountable"],"topics":["medicine","sciences"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/skəʊp/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/skoʊp/","tags":["Canada","General-American"]},{"ipa":"/skəʉp/","tags":["Australia"]},{"ipa":"[skɐʉp]","tags":["Australia"]},{"ipa":"/skɐʉp/","tags":["New-Zealand"]},{"ipa":"/sko(ː)p/","tags":["Caribbean","India","Scotland"]}],"upstream_line":271763,"upstream_offset":1357980897,"upstream_record_sha256":"dd0836383edc7b4dd8094f690ffd2bec164a65121835047997139c64d92f00f7","word":"scope"}
